## Executive Summary

#### Critical Windows of Complexity Control: When Transformers Decide to Reason or Memorize

问题与机制：regularization timing creates a critical training window that changes the reasoning-versus-memorization basin 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04396v1 §3 Methodology — mechanism: We show that the memorization-versus-reasoning fate of a Transformer is determined within a sharp, identifiable window of training.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04396v1 §4 Experimental Setup; §5 Results and Discussion — disclosed scope: We further show that the critical-window phenomenon is task-specific: it does not appear on grokking with modular arithmetic, where properly tuned constant weight decay matches scheduled weight decay.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04396v1 Appendix A.6; Appendix B.5 — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04396v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04418v1 §5 Experiments — disclosed scope: Evaluations on large-scale LLM architectures demonstrate that MACRO achieves highly competitive performance while rigorously preserving the theoretical guarantees of exact Riemannian optimization.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04418v1 §6 Discussion and Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04418v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04431v1 §5 Experiments — disclosed scope: Moreover, RFT-FM shows strong capability in detecting, diagnosing, and mitigating RFT failures.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04431v1 §6 Limitations and Future Work — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04431v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04446v1 §5 Experiments — disclosed scope: Finally, because routing and output objectives can conflict, Misrouter uses a two-phase optimization strategy that first steers routing and then optimizes harmful outputs while preserving routing stability.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04446v1 §6 Discussion and Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04446v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-WHEN-KV-MEETS-EMBEDDINGS-DYNAMIC-GPU-MEMORY-ALLOCATION-FOR-ACCELERATING-:start -->
#### When KV Meets Embeddings: Dynamic GPU Memory Allocation for Accelerating Generative Recommender Serving

问题与机制：embedding hot-cache and KV-cache allocation must be co-scheduled as one HBM control problem under tail-latency SLOs 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04450v1 §3 Motivation; §4 HLEM Design — mechanism: While online reallocation is required to close this gap, naive approaches introduce H2D refill traffic on the critical path, causing P99 SLO violations.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04450v1 §5 Implementation; §6 Evaluation — disclosed scope: Existing systems optimize them in isolation, overlooking that the optimal EMB-KV allocation ratio can shift by up to 0.35 across workload regimes, leaving 20-30\% latency improvement unrealized.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04450v1 §7 Discussion — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04450v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

#### Deployment-Relevant Alignment Cannot Be Inferred from Model-Level Evaluation Alone

问题与机制：deployment claims require interaction and scaffold evidence rather than model-only scores 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04454v1 §3 System-Level Alignment Evaluation Agenda — mechanism: We propose a system-level evaluation agenda: alignment profiles instead of single scores, fixed-scaffolding protocols for comparable interactional evaluation, and reporting templates that make the inferential distance between evaluation evidence and deployment claims explicit.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04454v1 §4 Evaluation Protocols — disclosed scope: Alignment evaluation in machine learning has largely become evaluation of models.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04454v1 §5 Limitations and Open Questions — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04454v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-STABILIZING-LLM-SUPERVISED-FINE-TUNING-VIA-EXPLICIT-DISTRIBUTIONAL-CONTR:start -->
#### Stabilizing LLM Supervised Fine-Tuning via Explicit Distributional Control

问题与机制：SFT can constrain distribution drift through moving trust-region anchors instead of accepting catastrophic forgetting as a downstream surprise 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04468v1 §3 Anchored Learning — mechanism: Motivated by this perspective, we propose Anchored Learning, a simple framework that explicitly controls distributional updates during offline fine-tuning via a dynamically evolving moving anchor.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04468v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: For example, while standard SFT suffers from over 53% performance degradation on iGSM and MedCalc, Anchored Learning slashes this drop to under 5% while maintaining near-optimal gains (e.g., 75.2% on iGSM).`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04468v1 §5 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04468v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-DATA-DEPENDENT-EXPLORATION-FOR-ONLINE-REINFORCEMENT-LEARNING-FROM-HUMAN-:start -->
#### Data-dependent Exploration for Online Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback

问题与机制：online preference learning should allocate exploration from historical uncertainty rather than unreliable on-policy estimates alone 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04477v1 §4 Algorithm and Theory — mechanism: In this paper, we propose data-dependent exploration for preference optimization (DEPO), a simple and scalable method that leverages historical data to construct an extra uncertainty bonus for high-uncertainty regions, encouraging exploration toward potentially high-value data.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04477v1 §5 Experiments — disclosed scope: Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estimate reliably from the limited historical preference data available during training; as a result, the policy can prematurely down-weight under-explored regions that may contain high-value behaviors.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04477v1 §6 Conclusion — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04477v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

#### CCL-D: A High-Precision Diagnostic System for Slow and Hang Anomalies in Large-Scale Model Training

问题与机制：collective slow/hang diagnosis needs rank probes, fault fingerprints and remediation ownership inside the training runtime 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04478v1 §3 CCL-D Design — mechanism: To address this, we propose CCL-D, a high-precision diagnostic system designed to detect and locate slow/hang anomalies in large-scale distributed training.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04478v1 §4 Implementation; §5 Evaluation — disclosed scope: Deployed on a 4,000-GPU cluster over one year, CCL-D achieved near-complete coverage of known slow/hang anomalies and pinpointed affected ranks within 6 minutes-substantially outperforming existing solutions.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04478v1 §6 Discussion — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04478v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-SECUREMCP-A-POLICY-ENFORCED-LLM-DATA-ACCESS-FRAMEWORK-FOR-AIOT-SYSTEMS-V:start -->
#### SecureMCP: A Policy-Enforced LLM Data Access Framework for AIoT Systems via Model Context Protocol

问题与机制：MCP data access must be policy-enforced at the effect boundary rather than delegated to model intent 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05260v1 https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05260v1 §3.3–3.5 — sequential fail-closed pipeline: table/column/operation RBAC, cost gate, SQL interceptor, risk classifier and database isolation own distinct pre-execution decisions — mechanism: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM)-generated SQL queries in Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems introduces critical security risks, as prompt injection attacks can manipulate LLMs into producing unauthorized queries that expose sensitive data or execute destructive operations.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05260v1 https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05260v1 §4.1–4.6; §5.1–5.5 — IoT-SQL subset, Qwen3-8B-FP8 via vLLM 0.8, A6000, four clean-query roles and 2,400 adversarial queries; reported results remain bound to that contract — disclosed scope: Experiment A demonstrates that defense modules preserve execution accuracy, with EX-in-ALLOW remaining within 65.1%-76.4% across four RBAC roles, matching the unprotected baseline of 63.8%.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05260v1 https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05260v1 §6.3–6.4 — single model and benchmark, researcher-designed attacks, auditor-only adversarial role, rule-based blind spots and a coarse global cost threshold — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05260v1 §4.6 — Python 3.11/sqlparse custom modules plus mysql-mcp-server-sse configuration are disclosed; immutable event-time implementation commit Not Disclosed`。

<!-- review:SF-MAXIMIZING-ROLLOUT-INFORMATIVENESS-UNDER-A-FIXED-BUDGET-A-SUBMODULAR-VIE:start -->
#### Maximizing Rollout Informativeness under a Fixed Budget: A Submodular View of Tree Search for Tool-Use Agentic Reinforcement Learning

问题与机制：tool-use rollout selection should optimize marginal information under a budget rather than expand a search tree uniformly 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05262v1 §4 Method: InfoTree — mechanism: We present InfoTree, a training-time tree-search framework coupling UUCB with a learned Adaptive Budget Allocator (ABA) and an asynchronous Speculative Expansion scheme.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05262v1 §5 Experiments — disclosed scope: Across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning (AIME 2024 and 2025, MATH-500, OlympiadBench, USAMO), web-search agents (GAIA, HLE-100, BrowseComp-lite), and tool-rich coding and OS agents (APPS-verified, AgentBench-OS), InfoTree outperforms flat GRPO, DeepSearch, Tree-GRPO, AT2PO, CW-GRPO, and RC-GRPO.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05262v1 §6 Conclusion; Broader Impact — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05262v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

问题与机制：approximate execution needs an explicit bounded-error correction contract rather than an average-accuracy claim 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04563v1 §V RangeGuard — mechanism: As DRAM scales in density and adopts 3D integration, raw fault rates increase and multi-bit errors are no longer rare.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04563v1 §VI Evaluation — disclosed scope: Large Language Models (LLMs) are particularly vulnerable because attention, residual, and normalization layers can amplify and preserve a single corrupted activation across many layers, destabilizing inference.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04563v1 §VII Conclusion — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04563v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-DREAM-MPC-GRADIENT-BASED-MODEL-PREDICTIVE-CONTROL-WITH-LATENT-IMAGINATIO:start -->
#### Dream-MPC: Gradient-Based Model Predictive Control with Latent Imagination

问题与机制：latent imagination is useful only when uncertainty and closed-loop MPC correction remain explicit 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04568v1 §4 Dream-MPC — mechanism: We propose Dream-MPC, a novel approach that generates few candidate trajectories from a rolled-out policy and optimizes each trajectory by gradient ascent using a learned world model, uncertainty regularization and amortization of optimization iterations over time by reusing previously optimized actions.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04568v1 §5 Experiments — disclosed scope: Our results on 24 continuous control tasks show that Dream-MPC can significantly improve the performance of the underlying policy and can outperform gradient-free MPC and state-of-the-art baselines.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04568v1 Appendix A Limitations and Future Work — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04568v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-FROM-PARAMETER-DYNAMICS-TO-RISK-SCORING-QUANTIFYING-SAMPLE-LEVEL-SAFETY-:start -->
#### From Parameter Dynamics to Risk Scoring : Quantifying Sample-Level Safety Degradation in LLM Fine-tuning

问题与机制：fine-tuning safety degradation can be localized to sample-level parameter dynamics and therefore audited during training 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04572v1 §3 Sample-Level Quantification of Safety Degradation — mechanism: Based on this insight, we propose a method of Sample-Level Quantification of Safety Degradation (SQSD), which quantifies the influence of each training sample on safety degradation.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04572v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that SQSD effectively quantifies sample-level fine-tuning risks and exhibits strong transferability across model architectures, parameter scales, and parameter-efficient methods.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04572v1 §5 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04572v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:start -->
#### DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap): A Controllable and Interactive Red-Teaming Platform for AI Agents

问题与机制：静态 prompt suite 在攻击面固定、工具没有真实副作用时仍是低成本回归；Agent 进入长轨迹、外部工具和可变环境后，评测对象必须升级为可重放的 environment transition。DTap 在 §3.1 把动态 state、deterministic transition、arbitrary-state reset、containerized tenant isolation 与 sandboxed MCP replica 绑定为环境 identity；§4.1–4.2 再以 prompt、tool、skill、environment 及其组合为 action space，由 effect-side verifiable judge 检查最终环境状态并反馈下一轮攻击搜索。这里改变的是 evaluation state 和 judge authority，不是把 attack agent 的叙述当真值。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04808v1 §5.1、§6.1–6.3` 披露 14 domains、50+ simulated environments、6,682 tasks，其中 1,998 indirect、1,878 direct、2,806 benign，并以 ASR/BSR 比较四个 agent frameworks、八个 model configurations。攻击序列先在 GPT-5.1 surrogate 上以最多 10 轮优化，再人工选择后做 zero-shot transfer；因此结果证明该 bounded harness 下多 surface 攻击和 outcome-state judge 可执行，不证明现实系统、未测模型或开放攻击空间具有相同 ASR。

Non-proof：exact-v1 没有独立 `Limitations` 章节；边界必须从 §5.1 与 §6.3 的披露反推。环境是 GUI/MCP replica 而非生产系统，attack set 受一个 surrogate、人工筛选和既定 policy taxonomy 约束；§6.3 对 batch/sequential harness 导致行为差异的解释明确使用 `conjecture`，不能升级为因果证明。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04808v1`；`https://github.com/AI-secure/DecodingTrust-Agent`；`https://decodingtrust-agent.com/docs`；event-time immutable commit `Not Disclosed`。
<!-- claim:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:start -->Agent red-team evaluation must bind replayable environment state transitions, injection and threat-model identity, and effect-side judges; DTap's simulated and surrogate-optimized results do not prove production transfer or complete attack coverage.<!-- claim:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:end -->
<!-- review:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:end -->

<!-- review:SF-TREE-BASED-CREDIT-ASSIGNMENT-FOR-MULTI-AGENT-MEMORY-SYSTEM:start -->
#### Tree-based Credit Assignment for Multi-Agent Memory System

问题与机制：multi-agent memory requires tree-structured credit assignment over shared and delegated state changes 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04811v1 §3 Method — mechanism: To empower this system, existing RL-based methods either apply final downstream task rewards (e.g., QA accuracy) for all agents uniformly, which are coarse and ambiguous, or design task-specific rewards for agents on different subtasks, which require costly annotations (e.g., key evidence) and…`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04811v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: Experiments on long-horizon benchmarks show that TreeMem improves memory system performance over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of tree-structured credit assignment for the multi-agent memory system.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04811v1 §5 Conclusion — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04811v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-STORAGE-IS-NOT-MEMORY-A-RETRIEVAL-CENTERED-ARCHITECTURE-FOR-AGENT-RECALL:start -->
#### Storage Is Not Memory: A Retrieval-Centered Architecture for Agent Recall

问题与机制：durable storage is not usable memory without retrieval, admission and update ownership 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04897v1 §3 Retrieval-Centered Memory Architecture — mechanism: We propose True Memory, a six-layer architecture that shifts the center of the system from a storage schema to a multi-stage retrieval pipeline operating over events preserved verbatim.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04897v1 §4 Evaluation — disclosed scope: On LoCoMo (1,540 questions across 10 multi-session conversations), True Memory Pro reaches 93.0% accuracy (3-run mean) against 61.4% for Mem0, 65.4% for Supermemory, approximately 71% for Zep, and 94.5% for EverMemOS under a matched gpt-4.1-mini answer model.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04897v1 §5 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04897v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-YOU-SNOOZE-YOU-LOSE-AUTOMATIC-SAFETY-ALIGNMENT-RESTORATION-THROUGH-NEURA:start -->
#### You Snooze, You Lose: Automatic Safety Alignment Restoration through Neural Weight Translation

问题与机制：safety alignment restoration after fine-tuning can be modeled as a weight-space repair operation with explicit regression risk 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04992v1 §3 Methodology — mechanism: We propose Neural Weight Translation (NeWTral), which maps unsafe domain adapters to their safety-aligned counterparts entirely within parameter space while preserving their expertise.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04992v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: Across four architectural families (Llama, Mistral, Qwen, Gemma) at scales up to 72B parameters and eight professional domains, NeWTral cuts the average Attack Success Rate from 70% to 13% while retaining 88% knowledge fidelity.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04992v1 §5 Limitations and Future Work — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.04992v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

#### Uno-Orchestra: Parsimonious Agent Routing via Selective Delegation

问题与机制：multi-agent routing should selectively delegate from task and uncertainty state rather than invoke a fixed team 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05007v1 §4 Selective Delegation Method — mechanism: We introduce Uno-Orchestra, a unified orchestration policy that selectively decomposes a task and dispatches each subtask to an admissible (model, primitive) pair, with both decisions learned together from curated RL trajectories grounded in real worker interactions.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05007v1 §5 Routing Policy; §6 Experiments — disclosed scope: Against 22 baselines on a 13-benchmark suite spanning math, code, knowledge, long-context, and agentic tool-use, Uno-Orchestra reaches 77.0% macro pass@1, roughly 16% above the strongest workflow baseline, at roughly an order of magnitude lower per-query cost, advancing the accuracy-efficiency frontier of selective…`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05007v1 §7 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05007v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

问题与机制：large MoE training needs resource-model-driven pipelined hybrid parallelism that co-owns expert placement and communication 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05049v1 §3 Resource Model; §4 Piper Design — mechanism: To characterize these challenges, we develop a mathematical model that quantifies memory, compute, and communication requirements for MoE configurations under various parallelization schemes, verified through micro-benchmarking, code instrumentation, and hardware profiling.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05049v1 §5 Implementation; §6 Evaluation — disclosed scope: However, training MoE models on HPC platforms is hindered by large memory footprints, frequent large-scale communication across heterogeneous networks, and severe workload imbalance.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05049v1 §7 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05049v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-THE-IMPOSSIBILITY-TRIANGLE-OF-LONG-CONTEXT-MODELING:start -->
#### The Impossibility Triangle of Long-Context Modeling

问题与机制：long-context design cannot simultaneously optimize capacity, generalization and efficient exact access without workload trade-offs 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05066v1 §3 Impossibility Triangle — mechanism: We identify and prove a fundamental trade-off governing long-sequence models: no model can simultaneously achieve (i) per-step computation independent of sequence length (Efficiency), (ii) state size independent of sequence length (Compactness), and (iii) the ability to recall a number of historical facts…`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05066v1 §4 Analysis; §5 Experiments — disclosed scope: Experiments on synthetic associative recall tasks with five representative architectures validate the theoretical bound: empirical recall capacity lies strictly below the information-theoretic limit, and no architecture escapes the triangle.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05066v1 §6 Discussion and Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05066v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-CONTINUAL-KNOWLEDGE-UPDATING-IN-LLM-SYSTEMS-LEARNING-THROUGH-MULTI-TIMES:start -->
#### Continual Knowledge Updating in LLM Systems: Learning Through Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics

问题与机制：continual knowledge requires distinct timescales for weights, retrieval state and episodic memory 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05097v1 §3 Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics — mechanism: This workshop article describes an early-stage conceptual design without experimental evaluation.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05097v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: This workshop article describes an early-stage conceptual design without experimental evaluation.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05097v1 §5 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05097v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-ROLLOUT-PASS-RATE-CONTROL-STEERING-BINARY-REWARD-RL-TOWARD-ITS-MOST-INFO:start -->
#### Rollout Pass-Rate Control: Steering Binary-Reward RL Toward Its Most Informative Regime

问题与机制：binary-reward RL should control rollout pass rate to keep sampling in an informative regime 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05112v1 §3 Rollout Pass-Rate Control — mechanism: We frame this as pass-rate control and show that the binary reward-side signal is strongest near a 50% rollout pass rate under four criteria: reward entropy, group-filtering survival, leave-one-out (RLOO) advantage energy under Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and success-failure pair count.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05112v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: On SWE-bench Verified, PS reaches the baseline high-score regime within evaluation variability while delivering 2.01x and 1.55x end-to-end wall-clock speedups on Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B; the 14B peak improves from 0.274 to 0.295.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05112v1 §5 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05112v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。
<!-- claim:SF-ROLLOUT-PASS-RATE-CONTROL-STEERING-BINARY-REWARD-RL-TOWARD-ITS-MOST-INFO:start -->长期结论仅限上述 exact-v1 披露边界；不外推到未测模型、硬件、并发、精度或 SLO。<!-- claim:SF-ROLLOUT-PASS-RATE-CONTROL-STEERING-BINARY-REWARD-RL-TOWARD-ITS-MOST-INFO:end -->
<!-- review:SF-ROLLOUT-PASS-RATE-CONTROL-STEERING-BINARY-REWARD-RL-TOWARD-ITS-MOST-INFO:end -->

<!-- review:SF-LONGSEEKER-ELASTIC-CONTEXT-ORCHESTRATION-FOR-LONG-HORIZON-SEARCH-AGENTS:start -->
#### LongSeeker: Elastic Context Orchestration for Long-Horizon Search Agents

问题与机制：long-horizon search needs elastic context orchestration across active, compressed and recoverable state 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05191v1 §3 Elastic Context Orchestration — mechanism: We propose that effective context management should be adaptive: parts of the agent's trajectory are maintained at different levels of detail depending on their current relevance to the task.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05191v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: Across four representative search benchmarks, LongSeeker achieves 61.5% on BrowseComp and 62.5% on BrowseComp-ZH, substantially outperforming Tongyi DeepResearch (43.2% and 46.7%) and AgentFold (36.2% and 47.3%).`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05191v1 §5 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05191v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。
<!-- claim:SF-LONGSEEKER-ELASTIC-CONTEXT-ORCHESTRATION-FOR-LONG-HORIZON-SEARCH-AGENTS:start -->长期结论仅限上述 exact-v1 披露边界；不外推到未测模型、硬件、并发、精度或 SLO。<!-- claim:SF-LONGSEEKER-ELASTIC-CONTEXT-ORCHESTRATION-FOR-LONG-HORIZON-SEARCH-AGENTS:end -->
<!-- review:SF-LONGSEEKER-ELASTIC-CONTEXT-ORCHESTRATION-FOR-LONG-HORIZON-SEARCH-AGENTS:end -->

<!-- review:SF-SECURING-THE-AGENT-VENDOR-NEUTRAL-MULTITENANT-ENTERPRISE-RETRIEVAL-AND-T:start -->
#### Securing the Agent: Vendor-Neutral, Multitenant Enterprise Retrieval and Tool Use

问题与机制：enterprise agent retrieval and tool use require tenant-scoped identity and effect authorization 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05287v1 §3 Vendor-Neutral Multitenant Architecture — mechanism: However, real enterprise environments introduce challenges largely absent from academic treatments and consumer-facing APIs: multiple tenants with heterogeneous data, strict access-control requirements, regulatory compliance, and cost pressures that demand shared infrastructure.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05287v1 §4 Retrieval and Tool Isolation; §5 Evaluation — disclosed scope: We formalize this gap and analyze additional shortcomings--including tool-mediated disclosure, context accumulation across turns, and client-side orchestration bypass--that arise when agentic systems conflate relevance with authorization.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05287v1 §6 Threats and Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05287v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。
<!-- claim:SF-SECURING-THE-AGENT-VENDOR-NEUTRAL-MULTITENANT-ENTERPRISE-RETRIEVAL-AND-T:start -->长期结论仅限上述 exact-v1 披露边界；不外推到未测模型、硬件、并发、精度或 SLO。<!-- claim:SF-SECURING-THE-AGENT-VENDOR-NEUTRAL-MULTITENANT-ENTERPRISE-RETRIEVAL-AND-T:end -->

<!-- review:SF-PARTIAL-EVIDENCE-BENCH-BENCHMARKING-AUTHORIZATION-LIMITED-EVIDENCE-IN-AG:start -->
#### Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems

问题与机制：agent evaluation must model authorization-limited evidence rather than assume universal access to ground truth 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05379v1 §3 Authorization-Limited Evidence Contract — mechanism: Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05379v1 §4 Benchmark Design; §5 Results — disclosed scope: Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05379v1 §6 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05379v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

<!-- review:SF-FROM-HISTORY-TO-STATE-CONSTANT-CONTEXT-SKILL-LEARNING-FOR-LLM-AGENTS:start -->
#### From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents

问题与机制：recurring procedures can move from growing prompts into learned modules while deterministic workflow state remains explicit 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05413v1 §3 History-to-State Skill Learning — mechanism: We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05413v1 §4 Experiments — disclosed scope: Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05413v1 §5 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05413v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。
<!-- claim:SF-FROM-HISTORY-TO-STATE-CONSTANT-CONTEXT-SKILL-LEARNING-FOR-LLM-AGENTS:start -->长期结论仅限上述 exact-v1 披露边界；不外推到未测模型、硬件、并发、精度或 SLO。<!-- claim:SF-FROM-HISTORY-TO-STATE-CONSTANT-CONTEXT-SKILL-LEARNING-FOR-LLM-AGENTS:end -->

#### Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure

问题与机制：authorization must propagate through delegation, aggregation and time, not attach only to the initiating identity 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05440v1 §3 Identity and Authorization Propagation — mechanism: The security discussion around agentic AI focuses heavily on prompt injection.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05440v1 §4 System Model; §5 Evaluation — disclosed scope: This paper argues that multi-agent systems also create a distinct authorization problem: maintaining authorization invariants as non-human principals retrieve data, delegate tasks, and synthesize results across changing boundaries.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05440v1 §6 Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05440v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

#### Nitsum: Serving Tiered LLM Requests with Adaptive Tensor Parallelism

问题与机制：tensor parallelism can become a runtime control surface jointly optimized with PD split and request scheduling 旧路径在低风险、短轨迹或固定 workload 下仍成立。Method locator：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05467v1 §3 Nitsum Design; §4 Adaptive TP Controller — mechanism: We present Nitsum, a distributed LLM serving system that treats tensor parallelism (TP) as a first-class runtime control surface rather than a static deployment choice.`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05467v1 §5 Implementation; §6 Evaluation — disclosed scope: Experiments on real traces and targeted microbenchmarks show that Nitsum improves SLO-compliant goodput over SoTA by up to 5.3 times.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05467v1 §7 Discussion and Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05467v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

Evaluation：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05527v1 §VI Experimental Evaluation — disclosed scope: Experimental results on multiple hardware platforms show that EdgeServing consistently outperforms representative baselines in both SLO violation ratio and P95 latency, enabled by early-exit mechanism, which expands the scheduling action space under tight latency constraints.`。Non-proof：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05527v1 §VII Limitations — no generalization beyond disclosed model, workload, hardware, precision and SLO`。Artifact：`https://arxiv.org/html/2605.05527v1 — artifact/code statement; immutable event-time commit Not Disclosed unless explicitly named`。

本日只扩写三个跨层控制变化；未选 family 仍保留完整 Source Review 与 Books Decision，未被一笔带过。

<!-- analysis:DA-RFT-FAILURE-MANAGEMENT:start -->
### RFT 从算法稳定性推进到 Failure Management Control Loop

旧路径依赖 loss/KL/entropy 曲线和人工诊断，在故障稀少、训练规模小且重跑便宜时合理。RFT-FM 把多变量轨迹变成 fault fingerprint，再将 detection、attribution、intervention 与 revalidation 串成控制环。收益是故障状态可观测、可分类；代价是 classifier 与 remediation policy 自身成为版本化状态。作者 hard-setting attribution 与 46.25% remediation 结果同时给出反证：自动修复远未成为安全 commit owner，失败时必须停止并回退人工诊断/重跑。
<!-- analysis:DA-RFT-FAILURE-MANAGEMENT:end -->

<!-- analysis:DA-JOINT-HBM-CONTROL:start -->
### HBM 从单一 Cache 调优推进到共同资源池

只优化 KV 或 embedding cache 在单一 workload 下合理；生成式推荐同时让两者竞争 HBM，静态比例会随序列和热门 embedding 分布失效。HLEM 让 allocator、background refill 和 router共同观察 KV residency、embedding locality 与 load。它换来更低 P99，却引入 online controller、refill bandwidth 和 stale-residency failure；普通 LLM 无 embedding table 竞争时不应照搬结论。
<!-- analysis:DA-JOINT-HBM-CONTROL:end -->

<!-- analysis:DA-DYNAMIC-TP-CONTROL:start -->
### Tensor Parallelism 从部署常量推进到 Runtime Control Surface

静态 TP 在 workload 稳定、迁移昂贵时最简单。Nitsum 通过常驻多 TP execution process、权重复用和 pipelined KV migration，把 TP、PD allocation 与 tiered-SLO scheduling放进一个秒级控制环。收益绑定 Llama/DeepSeek、A100/H100、16-bit、披露 trace 与自定义 SLO；额外全量权重驻留、warm process、profile stale 和 KV migration consistency 是新成本，宽松 SLO 下低 TP 静态配置仍可能更优。
<!-- analysis:DA-DYNAMIC-TP-CONTROL:end -->

<!-- existing:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:start -->第 66 章的“Trajectory Judge 必须区分叙述、动作与完成证据”已把 agent narrative、typed action、environment transition 与 task-specific effect check 排成证据序；随后的 simulator/EvalSpec 主线已经要求冻结 initial state、tool schema、fault policy 与 verifier。第 72 章的“Safety Evaluation 的单位是 Run”与 containment 分层又覆盖跨 prompt/tool/skill/environment channel 的攻击 identity 及 proposal/authorization/commit 边界。DTap exact-v1 的 state reset、simulated MCP、closed-loop attack search 与 outcome-state judge 是这些命题的受限实现，不改变 canonical owner。<!-- existing:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:end -->
<!-- delta:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:start -->DTap provides a bounded case of replayable stateful red-team environments, multi-surface attack search and effect-side judges; its surrogate-optimized benchmark does not change the existing evaluation ownership contract.<!-- delta:SF-DECODINGTRUST-AGENT-PLATFORM-DTAP-A-CONTROLLABLE-AND-INTERACTIVE-RED-TEA:end --> Decision: `No Change — Existing Coverage`；无需新增 Books 写回。
